  body: (record Production.ThreatModel::ModeledThreat {threat := "Security::threatUploadSessionAnswerThatBreaksINV001", name := "Answer that breaks INV-001", text := "The guest answers committed although label is mismatch and state is not committed, which matters because content that does not match its label is never committed.", category := (ctor Production.ThreatModel::Stride.repudiation)})
}

definition{
  name: modeledThreatUploadSessionAnswerThatBreaksINV002,
  parameters: [],
  result: Production.ThreatModel::ModeledThreat,
  body: (record Production.ThreatModel::ModeledThreat {threat := "Security::threatUploadSessionAnswerThatBreaksINV002", name := "Answer that breaks INV-002", text := "The guest answers abandoned not when event is timeout and state is not committed and state is not rejected, which matters because an expired session always releases its pin.", category := (ctor Production.ThreatModel::Stride.repudiation)})
}

definition{
  name: modeledThreatUploadSessionAnswerThatBreaksINV003,
  parameters: [],
  result: Production.ThreatModel::ModeledThreat,
  body: (record Production.ThreatModel::ModeledThreat {threat := "Security::threatUploadSessionAnswerThatBreaksINV003", name := "Answer that breaks INV-003", text := "The guest answers committed not when state is committed, which matters because committed is a final state (UML 2.5.1 FinalState): a run that reaches it stays there.", category := (ctor Production.ThreatModel::Stride.repudiation)})
}

definition{
  name: modeledThreatUploadSessionAnswerThatBreaksINV004,

}

}

definition{
  name: strideCategories,
  parameters: [],
  result: (List Nat),
  body: (cons 0 (cons 1 (cons 2 (cons 3 (cons 4 (cons 5 (nil Nat)))))))
}

definition{
  name: strideApplies,
  parameters: [kind : Nat, category : Nat],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (kind =? 0) then ((category =? 0) || (category =? 2)) else (if (kind =? 1) then (category <? 6) else (if (kind =? 2) then ((0 <? category) && (category <? 5)) else ((category =? 1) || ((category =? 3) || (category =? 4))))))
}

definition{
  name: runListed,
  parameters: [element : Nat, category : Nat, pairs : (List StridePair)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match pairs
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons pair rest => (if ((project pair element) =? element) then (((project pair category) =? category) || (runListed element category rest)) else false)),
  recursive_argument: pairs
}

definition{
  name: afterRun,
  parameters: [element : Nat, pairs : (List StridePair)],
  result: (List StridePair),
  body: (match pairs
    | List.nil => (nil StridePair)
    | List.cons pair rest => (if ((project pair element) =? element) then (afterRun element rest) else (cons pair rest))),
  recursive_argument: pairs
}

definition{
  name: pairsEmpty,
  parameters: [pairs : (List StridePair)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match pairs | List.nil => true | List.cons pair rest => false)
}

definition{
  name: exactlyOne,
  parameters: [left : Bool, right : Bool],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if left then (if right then false else true) else right)
}

definition{
  name: categoriesStated,
  parameters: [
    element : Nat,
    kind : Nat,
    answered : (List StridePair),
    stated : (List StridePair),
    categories : (List Nat)
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match categories
    | List.nil => true
    | List.cons category rest => ((if (strideApplies kind category) then (exactlyOne (runListed element category answered) (runListed element category stated)) else (if (runListed element category stated) then false else true)) && (categoriesStated element kind answered stated rest))),
  recursive_argument: categories
}

definition{
  name: elementsStated,
  parameters: [
    position : Nat,
    records : (List StrideElement),
    answered : (List StridePair),
    stated : (List StridePair)
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match records
    | List.nil => ((pairsEmpty answered) && (pairsEmpty stated))
    | List.cons item rest => (((project item index) =? position) && ((categoriesStated position (project item kind) answered stated (strideCategories)) && (elementsStated (position + 1) rest (afterRun position answered) (afterRun position stated))))),
  recursive_argument: records
}

definition{
  name: pairsKnown,
  parameters: [count : Nat, pairs : (List StridePair)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match pairs
    | List.nil => true
    | List.cons pair rest => (((project pair element) <? count) && (((project pair category) <? 6) && (pairsKnown count rest)))),
  recursive_argument: pairs
}

definition{
  name: pairCount,
  parameters: [values : (List StridePair)],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match values | List.nil => 0 | List.cons value rest => ((pairCount rest) + 1)),
  recursive_argument: values
}

definition{
  name: securityValid,
  parameters: [],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((elementsStated 0 (strideElements) (threatPairs) (openPairs)) && ((pairsKnown 113 (threatPairs)) && (pairsKnown 113 (openPairs))))
}

theorem{
  name: strideEnumerated,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((securityValid) = true),
  proof: decide{}
}

theorem{
  name: strideOpenCount,
  parameters: [],
  statement: ((pairCount (openPairs)) = 257),
  proof: decide{}
}
\end{semanticmodule}
\end{lexlean}
